BY DIRECTOR OF TOWN PLANNING
Maharashtra Regional Planning and Town Planning Act, 1966
NOTIFICATION
No. Final T.P. Scheme Pachora No. 1/T.P. Pachora/Dist. Jalgaon/Section 86(1)(b)/TPV-4/4444/
2026.– Whereas, the Draft Town Planning Scheme submitted by the Pachora Municipal Council for
approval vide their letter No. 1552, dated 14th October 2024 (hereinafter referred to as ‘the said
Draft Scheme’) has been sanctioned by the Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune
vide Notiﬁ cation No. TPS/Pachora T.P. Scheme/Sanction/68(2)/TPV-4/6346, dated 3rd December 2024
under sub-section (2) of the Section 68 of the Maharashtra Regional Planning and Town Planning
Act, 1966 (hereinafter referred to as ‘the said Act’) as per the power delegated under Section 151
(1) of the the said Act ;
and whereas, the Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune vide Notiﬁ cation No. TPS/
Pachora T.P. Scheme/Arbitrator/Section-72(1)/TPV-4/6608/2024 dated 16th December 2024 issued
under sub-section (1) of Section 72 of the said Act, has appointed Shri. Manohar Y. Bhargave, Retired
Assistant Director of Town Planning as the Arbitrator in respect of the said Draft Scheme (hereinafter
referred to as ‘the said Arbitrator’) ;
and whereas, the said Arbitrator, after following the prescribed procedure as required under sub-
section (3) and (4) of Section 72 of the said Act, has drawn up in the prescribed form, the Preliminary
Town Planning Scheme, Pachora No. 1 in accordance with the said sanctioned Draft Scheme and
submitted for sanction under sub-section (5) of Section 72 of the said Act, to the Director of Town
Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune for sanction vide letter No. 27, dated 30th June 2025 ;
and whereas, the Preliminary Town Planning Scheme, Pachora No. 1 submitted by the said
Arbitrator has been sanctioned by the Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune vide
Notiﬁ cation No. Preliminary T.P. Scheme, Pachora No. 1/Pachora/Dist. Jalgaon/Section 86(1)(c)/TPV-
4/4847, dated 18th August 2025 under sub-section (1)(a) of the Section 86 of the said Act, as per
the power delegated under Section 151 (1) of the the said Act ;

and whereas, the said Arbitrator, after following the prescribed procedure as required under sub-
sections (3), (5), (6) and (7) of Section 72 of the said Act, has drawn up in the prescribed form, the
Final Town Planning Scheme, Pachora No. 1 in accordance with the said sanctioned Draft Scheme ;
and whereas, as no appeal has been received by the said Arbitrator in respect of the said
Scheme, the Appellate Tribunal has not been constituted in accordance with the provisions of Section
75 of the said Act ;
and whereas, the said Arbitrator, after completing the necessary statutory procedure in respect
of the said Scheme, has submitted the Final Town Planning Scheme, Pachora No. 1 to the Director
of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune, for approval vide Letter No. 55, dated 30th July 2026,
in accordance with Sections 72 (5) and 82 (2) of the said Act (hereinafter referred to as ‘the said
Final Town Planning Scheme’) ;
and whereas, in accordance with the provisions of sub-section (1)(b) of Section 86 of the said
Act, the said Final Town Planning Scheme is required to be sanctioned or rejected within a period
of three months from the date of receipt of the said Final Town Planning Scheme from the Arbitrator
under Section 82 (1)(b) of the said Act ;
and whereas, the Government of Maharashtra in Urban Development Department vide it’s
Notiﬁ cation No. TPS-1813/3211/CR-508/UD-13, dated 1st December 2016 has delegated the powers
exercisable by it, under Section 86 (1) and (2) of the said Act, the powers of the Government to
sanction the Final Town Planning Scheme in respect of all Municipal Councils / Nagarpanchayats to
the Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune in accordance with the provisions of Section
151, sub-section (1) of the said Act ;
and whereas, after carrying out the necessary scrutiny, the Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra
State, Pune is of the opinion that the Final Town Planning Scheme, Pachora No. 1 is required to be
sanctioned.
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers delegated to him under sub-section (1) (b) of Section
86 of the said Act, and all other powers enabling in this behalf, the Director of Town Planning,
Maharashtra State, Pune hereby :–
(a) Sanctions the said Final Town Planning Scheme, Pachora No. 1 as proposed by the said
Arbitrator without any modiﬁ cations.
(b) Directs that, this Notiﬁ cation and the said Final Town Planning Scheme, Pachora No. 1 as
sanctioned shall be kept open for inspection by the public for a period of six months during
oﬃ ce hours on all working days in the Oﬃ ce of Municipal Council, Pachora.
(c) Directs that, the copies of the said Final Scheme or extracts there from certiﬁ ed to be correct
shall be available for sale to the public on application at a reasonable price at the oﬃ ce of
the Municipal Council, Pachora.
(d) Fixes dated 4th September 2026 to be the date on which all the liabilities created shall
take eﬀ ect and the said Final Scheme i.e. Final Town Planning Scheme, Pachora No.1 as
sanctioned shall come into force.
(e) This notiﬁ cation shall also be available for inspection of general public during oﬃ ce hours
on all working days for the period of one month in the following oﬃ ces :
(i) Oﬃ ce of the Arbitrator, Town Planning Scheme, Pachora No.1, Pachora, district Jalgaon.
(ii) Joint Director of the Town Planning, Nashik Division, Nashik.
(iii) Assistant Director of Town Planning, Jalgaon Branch, district Jalgaon.
(f) This notiﬁ cation is made available on the www.dtp.maharashtra.gov.in website of Director
of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune.
JITENDRA L. BHOPLE,
Director of Town Planning,
Pune, 25th August 2026. Maharashtra State, Pune.
